## The ticket

Make a multi-turn agent conversation a durable, resumable record: the seed becomes a real logged turn, resume state is derived from the log instead of a step parity, and the resumed run neither re-generates nor re-counts what it already has.

### New module `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/turn_ledger.py`

- Module docstring: `"""The durable record of a multi-turn agent conversation."""`. It takes every timestamp as a parameter — it imports neither `time`, `random`, `uuid` nor `os.urandom`.
- Constants: `RESPONSES_FILENAME: str = "responses_0.jsonl"`, `SEED_FINISH_REASON: str = "seed"`, `COMPLETION_SENTINEL: str`, `COMPLETION_REASONS: tuple[str, ...] = ("open", "budget", "agent_signal")`, `LEDGER_STATUSES: tuple[str, ...] = ("created", "adopted", "verified")`.
- Exceptions: `TurnLedgerError(RuntimeError)` as the base for every turn-ledger failure, and `TurnLedgerCorruptError(TurnLedgerError)` with `__init__(self, path: str, line_number: int, reason: str)` storing `.path`, `.line_number` (1-based over physical lines) and `.reason`.
- Two **frozen dataclasses** (`@dataclass(frozen=True)` — not pydantic models, not `NamedTuple`s, not dicts), field order exactly:
  - `TurnEntry(turn, author, content, source)` — `turn` is the 0-based index into the log (the seed is `0`), `author` is the agent name the line was written under, `content` is `response_message` coerced with `str()` when it is not already a `str`, `source` is `"seed"` for turn 0 and `"response"` otherwise.
  - `TurnLedger(entries, seeder_name, partner_name, max_responses, responses, turns, next_speaker, last_author, interleave_faults, completed, completion_reason, status)`, where `entries` is a `tuple[TurnEntry, ...]` and never a `list`.
- `TurnLedger.messages() -> list[dict[str, str]]` returns `[{"role": entry.author, "content": entry.content}, ...]` in log order.
- Functions:
  - `build_seed_record(*, seeder_name: str, seed_message: str, model_name: str, now: datetime.datetime) -> AgentResponse`
  - `read_log(working_dir: str) -> list[AgentResponse]`
  - `build_ledger(records: t.Sequence[AgentResponse], *, seeder_name: str, partner_name: str, max_responses: int, is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool], status: str = "verified") -> TurnLedger`
  - `load_ledger(working_dir: str, *, seeder_name: str, partner_name: str, max_responses: int, is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool]) -> TurnLedger` — loads the ledger for a working directory and never writes to disk.

### Ledger semantics

- `responses == len(entries) - 1`, never negative; `turns == len(entries)`.
- `next_speaker` is the agent that did **not** write the last entry: `partner_name if last_author == seeder_name else seeder_name`. It is never computed from `len(entries) % 2`, from `responses % 2`, or from a step counter. An entry whose author is neither `seeder_name` nor `partner_name` counts as "not the seeder", so `next_speaker` becomes `seeder_name`.
- A log in which the same author appears twice in a row is **legal**: it does not raise, does not truncate the log, and does not drop the duplicate. `interleave_faults` is the number of indices `i >= 1` with `entries[i].author == entries[i - 1].author`, and the next turn still goes to the complement of the last author.
- `completion_reason` is `"agent_signal"` when the **last** entry's author's `is_completed` returns true for its content (checked on the last entry only, not on every entry); otherwise `"budget"` when `responses >= max_responses`; otherwise `"open"`. `"agent_signal"` wins when both hold. `completed` is `completion_reason != "open"`, and `next_speaker is None` exactly when `completed` is true.
- An empty log gives `(completion_reason, responses, turns, last_author, next_speaker) == ("open", 0, 0, None, None)` with `completed is False`.
- `read_log` skips lines that are empty or whitespace-only and raises `TurnLedgerCorruptError(path, line_number, reason)` for any other unparseable line, instead of letting pydantic's `ValidationError` out.

### `MultiTurnAgenticProcessor` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/processor.py`)

- `__init__(self, seeder, partner, max_length, seed_message, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now)`; keeps `self.now_fn` and `self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None`. `AgentStatusTracker(max_turns=self.max_length)` is unchanged.
- `load_cache(self, working_dir: str) -> TurnLedger` returns a `TurnLedger` and does not touch the status tracker at all. The processor keeps the last ledger it built on `self.ledger`.
- **The seed is a durable turn.** When `responses_0.jsonl` is absent or empty, `run()` writes exactly one line — `build_seed_record(...)` — through the existing `append_response()` *before* the first request is built, then enters the loop. That record has `name = seeder.name`, `response_message = seed_message`, `finish_reason = "seed"`, `response_cost = 0.0`, `token_usage = None`, `response_errors = None`, `raw_response = None`, `raw_request = None`, `parsed_response_message = None`, `created_at = finished_at = now_fn()`, and `generic_request = GenericRequest(model=seeder.model_name, messages=[{"role": "user", "content": seed_message}], original_row={"prompt": seed_message}, original_row_idx=0, response_format=None, generation_params={}, is_multimodal_prompt=False)`.
- **`max_length` is a budget of generated responses, not of messages.** The seed is not one. The loop condition is `while ledger.responses < self.max_length`, so an un-stopped run makes exactly `max_length` calls to `call_single_request` and leaves `max_length + 1` log lines whether it ran once or resumed five times. `APIRequest(task_id=...)` is given `ledger.responses`, the 0-based response index.
- Each iteration asks the agent named by `ledger.next_speaker`; `_agent_for(name)` maps a name to `seeder`/`partner` and raises `KeyError` otherwise. `run()` short-circuits on `ledger.completed`, never on a message count reaching `max_length`.
- `self.conversation_history` stays a `list[dict[str, str]]` keyed `"role"` (an agent *name*) and `"content"`, and equals `self.ledger.messages()` after every ledger update.
- `run()` calls `status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)` exactly once, immediately after the ledger is loaded and **before** the `ledger.completed` short-circuit.
- A failed turn appends nothing to the log, is recorded against the agent named by `ledger.next_speaker` (not by step parity), and the exception is still re-raised.
- `_transform_conversation_history(target_agent)` maps every ledger message to `{"role": "assistant"}` when its author equals `target.name` and `{"role": "user"}` otherwise — uniformly, with **no special case for a one-message history**. It calls `target.prompt_formatter.create_generic_request({"prompt": <content of the last message>}, 0)`, splits that result into the system message and the rest, replaces the last mapped message with the rest, and inserts the system message at index 0 **only if there is one**; an agent built without `system_prompt=` must not raise. When the formatter produced more than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in place in the body.
- `create_dataset_file(working_dir)` writes one row per log line with exactly four fields: `{"role": <the record's name>, "content": <str(response_message)>, "turn": <0-based line index>, "source": "seed" if index == 0 else "response"}`. `source` is decided by position, not by `finish_reason`. Row order is log order.

### `AgentStatusTracker` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/agent_status_tracker.py`)

- New fields `num_cached: int = 0` and `time_fn: t.Callable[[], float] = field(default=time.time, repr=False, compare=False)`; `start_time` and `last_update_time` come from `time_fn`.
- `update_turn(agent, response_success=False, ...)` increments **only** `num_errors` — `current_turn` and `num_responses` are left alone — while still setting `current_agent` and still accumulating any token/cost arguments given.
- New `adopt_ledger(self, *, cached_responses: int) -> None`: sets `current_turn = cached_responses`, `num_cached = cached_responses`, `num_responses = 0`, `num_errors = 0`, `total_cost = 0.0` and all three `total_tokens` counters to `0`; it does not touch `max_turns` and does not call `update_display`. So `num_responses`/`total_cost` describe *this process*, `num_cached` describes what was inherited, and `current_turn` describes the conversation.
- `stop_tracker()` removes `"time_fn"` from the telemetry metadata dict alongside `"pbar"`.

### `Agent` / `MultiTurnAgents` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent.py`)

- `Agent.is_completed(self, response: t.Any) -> bool` currently returns `False` unconditionally, which makes the processor's stop-condition path dead code in every stock use. Give it a real default implementation in terms of `COMPLETION_SENTINEL`.
- `MultiTurnAgents.__init__(self, seeder, partner, max_length, seed_message, *, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now)` accepts and forwards `now_fn`.

### `MultiTurnResponse` (`src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/agent_response.py`)

- `update_tracker_stats()` reports `RequestStats(total=tracker.max_turns, succeeded=tracker.num_responses, failed=tracker.num_errors, in_progress=0, cached=tracker.num_cached)`.

### Reuse, scope and dependencies

- Reuse `AgentResponse` and its `model_validate_json`/`model_dump` (the seed is written as an `AgentResponse`, no new record type), `GenericRequest`, `MultiTurnAgenticProcessor.append_response()` verbatim, `PromptFormatter.create_generic_request()` (read, never modified), `AgentTurn`, `_TokenUsage`, `ArrowWriter`/`Dataset.from_file` as already used, and `APIRequest` constructed exactly as today.
- Nothing in `llm/`, `request_processor/`, `client.py`, `db.py` or the viewer changes. `Agent._hash_fingerprint` and the `xxh64(seed_message)` run identity are untouched.
- Python `^3.10`; **no new dependency** — `pydantic`, `datasets`, `aiofiles`, `aiohttp` and stdlib `json`/`os`/`datetime`/`typing` are all already present. Tests use `pytest`, `pytest-asyncio`, `tmp_path`, `monkeypatch` and `types.SimpleNamespace`; no network, no real provider.

## Requirements settled earlier

These were agreed before the ticket was written. They are not optional.

### r1

- **rule** — A JSON checkpoint sits in the same working directory: module constants `TURN_LEDGER_FILENAME: str = "turn_ledger.json"` and `TURN_LEDGER_VERSION: int = 2`, a `TurnLedger.sidecar_state() -> dict[str, t.Any]` returning exactly the eight keys `{"version": TURN_LEDGER_VERSION, "responses": int, "turns": int, "last_author": str | None, "next_speaker": str | None, "interleave_faults": int, "completed": bool, "completion_reason": str}`, and `write_sidecar(working_dir, ledger) -> str` (returns the absolute path). The file is serialized as `json.dumps(ledger.sidecar_state(), indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"`, and `run()` rewrites it immediately after the seed line and again after **every** appended response.
- **scope** — `load_ledger` is `read_log` + `build_ledger` + `verify_sidecar` and writes nothing; the `status` a ledger carries comes from that verification (`"created"` for a fresh run's first write, `"adopted"` or `"verified"` after a load). No log line is ever truncated, ignored, re-ordered or rewritten, and only two of the recorded keys are compared on load — `"responses"` and `"last_author"`.
- **failure_behavior** — `verify_sidecar` has exactly three arms. (a) The file is absent, or unreadable as JSON, or its `"version"` is not `TURN_LEDGER_VERSION` → `status = "adopted"`. (b) The version matches **and** both `"responses"` and `"last_author"` equal the log-derived values → `status = "verified"`. (c) The version matches and either value differs → raise `TurnLedgerDesyncError(path, log_responses, recorded_responses, log_last_author, recorded_last_author)`, a subclass of `TurnLedgerError`, storing all five as attributes of those names, with message `f"{path} records {recorded_responses} response(s) last authored by {recorded_last_author!r}, the log holds {log_responses} last authored by {log_last_author!r}"`. The raise happens before any request is issued and before anything is appended.
- **observability** — Fresh run in `tmp_path` with a fake `call_single_request` that raises on its first call: `tmp_path/"turn_ledger.json"` already exists, is exactly 186 bytes (trailing newline included), and reads `{"completed": false, "completion_reason": "open", "interleave_faults": 0, "last_author": "client", "next_speaker": "advisor", "responses": 0, "turns": 1, "version": 2}` in that sorted, 2-space-indented spelling. After a completed 4-line run the file is 189 bytes and `read_sidecar(str(tmp_path)) == {"version": 2, "responses": 3, "turns": 4, "last_author": "advisor", "next_speaker": None, "interleave_faults": 0, "completed": True, "completion_reason": "agent_signal"}`. Truncating that log to its first 3 lines and leaving the stale file makes a new processor's `run()` raise `TurnLedgerDesyncError` with `.path == str(tmp_path/"turn_ledger.json")`, `.log_responses == 2`, `.recorded_responses == 3`, `.log_last_author == "client"`, `.recorded_last_author == "advisor"`, zero calls to `call_single_request` and the log still 3 lines; deleting the file instead gives `ledger.status == "adopted"` and exactly one further call. A sidecar of `{"version": 1, "responses": 2, "last_author": "client"}` against that same 3-line log also gives `"adopted"`.

### r2

- **rule** — `COMPLETION_SENTINEL` is exactly the string `"<<END_OF_CONVERSATION>>"`, and the base `Agent.is_completed(response)` returns `True` iff `response.rstrip().endswith(COMPLETION_SENTINEL)`.
- **scope** — Matching is case-sensitive and suffix-only, and insensitive to trailing whitespace.
- **failure_behavior** — A non-`str` `response_message` returns `False` rather than raising.
- **observability** — The message carrying the sentinel is a real turn: it is appended to `responses_0.jsonl`, counted in `responses`, and present in the dataset, and the conversation stops after it. End to end with `max_length = 6` and a fake whose third reply is `"Then index funds. <<END_OF_CONVERSATION>>"`: the fake is called exactly `3` times, the log holds `4` lines, `tracker.num_responses == 3`, `ledger.completion_reason == "agent_signal"`, and the dataset's last row's `content` is `"Then index funds. <<END_OF_CONVERSATION>>"`.

</problem>

[msg for msg in request.messages if msg[\\"role\\"] == \\"system\\"][0]\\n        request.messages = [msg for msg in request.messages if msg[\\"role\\"] != \\"system\\"]\\n\\n        del transformed_conversation_history[-1]\\n        transformed_conversation_history += request.messages\\n        request.messages = transformed_conversation_history\\n        request.messages.insert(0, system_prompt)\\n        return request\\n\\n    def create_dataset_file(self, working_dir: str) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create a dataset file from the conversation history.\\n\\n        This method converts the JSONL conversation history into an Arrow dataset file\\n        for efficient storage and processing.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            working_dir (str): Directory where the dataset file will be created.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            str: Path to the created dataset file.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        response_file = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"responses_0.jsonl\\")\\n        dataset_file = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"dataset.arrow\\")\\n\\n        with ArrowWriter(path=dataset_file) as writer:\\n            with open(response_file, \\"r\\") as f_in:\\n                for line in f_in:\\n                    response = AgentResponse.model_validate_json(line)\\n                    row = response.model_dump()\\n                    response = {\\"content\\": row[\\"response_message\\"], \\"role\\": row[\\"name\\"]}\\n                    # Write the row to the arrow file\\n                    writer.write(response)\\n\\n            # Finalize the writer\\n            writer.finalize()\\n\\n        return dataset_file",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] \\"Seeder and partner must have different names\\"\\n        self._processor = MultiTurnAgenticProcessor(self.seeder, self.partner, self.max_length, self.seed_message)\\n\\n    def \\"response_format\\": \\"N/A\\",\\n            \\"batch_mode\\": False,\\n            \\"status\\": \\"COMPLETED\\",\\n            \\"timestamp\\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\\n        }\\n\\n    def _handle_viewer_push(self, viewer_client: Client, dataset, metadata_dict: dict, working_dir: str) -> str | None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Handle pushing the dataset to the curator viewer.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            viewer_client (Client): The curator viewer client.\\n            dataset: The dataset to push.\\n            metadata_dict (dict): Metadata for the viewer.\\n existing session\\n        metadata_db_path = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"metadata.db\\")\\n        metadata_db = MetadataDB(metadata_db_path)\\n        existing_session_id = metadata_db.get_existing_session_id(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n        existing_viewer_sync = metadata_db.check_existing_hosted_sync(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n\\n        if not existing_viewer_sync and existing_session_id:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict)\\n        else:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict, session_id=existing_session_id)\\n\\n        metadata_dict[\\"session_id\\"] = session_id\\n        metadata_dict[\\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"] = False\\n        metadata_db.store_metadata(metadata_dict)\\n\\n        # Push dataset to viewer\\n        if existing_session_id is None:\\n            # No existing session, push the dataset\\n {metadata_dict[\'run_hash\']}. \\"\\n                \\"The dataset has already been pushed to the Curator Viewer. \\"\\n                \\"If you want to push a new version, please use a different run hash.\\"\\n            )\\n            logger.warning(msg)\\n\\n        metadata_db.update_sync_viewer_flag(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"], True)\\n        return viewer_client.curator_viewer_url\\n\\n    def __call__(self, working_dir: t.Optional[str] = None) -> MultiTurnResponse:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Execute the multi-turn conversation between the agents.\\n\\n        This method runs the conversation simulation and saves the results\\n        in the specified working directory. If no working directory is provided,\\n        it uses the CURATOR_CACHE_DIR environment variable or a default location.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            working_dir (Optional[str]): The directory where conversation results\\n                will be stored. If None, uses environment variable or default.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            MultiTurnResponse: A response object containing the conversation results and statistics.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if working_dir is None:\\n            working_dir = os.environ.get(\\n                \\"CURATOR_CACHE_DIR\\",\\n                os.path.expanduser(_CURATOR_DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR),\\n            )\\n        disable_cache = os.getenv(\\"CURATOR_DISABLE_CACHE\\", \\"\\").lower() in [\\"true\\", \\"1\\"]\\n        fingerprint = self.seeder._hash_fingerprint(disable_cache=disable_cache)\\n        fingerprint += xxh64(self.seed_message).hexdigest()\\n        fingerprint += self.partner._hash_fingerprint(disable_cache=disable_cache)\\n        working_dir = os.path.join(working_dir, fingerprint)\\n        os.makedirs(working_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n        logger.info(f\\"Running multi turn simulation, find results in {working_dir}\\")\\n\\n        viewer_client = Client()\\n        self._processor.viewer_client = viewer_client\\n\\n        # Create metadata database\\n        metadata_db_path = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"metadata.db\\")\\n        metadata_db = MetadataDB(metadata_db_path)\\n\\n        # Run the conversation and get the dataset\\n        dataset = run_in_event_loop(self._processor.run(working_dir=working_dir))\\n\\n        # Create and populate the response object\\n        response = MultiTurnResponse(\\n            dataset=dataset,\\n            cache_dir=working_dir,\\n            seeder_model=self.seeder.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n            partner_model=self.partner.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n            metadata={\\n                \\"max_length\\": self.max_length,\\n                \\"seeder_name\\": self.seeder.name,\\n                \\"partner_name\\": self.partner.name,\\n                \\"seed_message\\": self.seed_message,\\n                \\"timestamp\\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\\n                \\"run_hash\\": fingerprint,\\n            },\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update statistics from the processor\'s status tracker\\n        response.update_tracker_stats(self._processor.status_tracker)\\n\\n        # Setup metadata for viewer\\n        metadata_dict = self._setup_metadata(fingerprint)\\n        existing_session_id = metadata_db.get_existing_session_id(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n        existing_viewer_sync = metadata_db.check_existing_hosted_sync(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"])\\n\\n        if not existing_viewer_sync and existing_session_id:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict)\\n        else:\\n            session_id = viewer_client.create_session(metadata_dict, session_id=existing_session_id)\\n\\n        metadata_dict[\\"session_id\\"] = session_id\\n        metadata_dict[\\"is_hosted_viewer_synced\\"] = False\\n        metadata_db.store_metadata(metadata_dict)\\n\\n        # Handle viewer push if enabled\\n        if viewer_client.hosted:\\n            viewer_url = self._handle_viewer_push(viewer_client, dataset, metadata_dict, working_dir)\\n            response.viewer_url = viewer_url\\n            if viewer_url:\\n                logger.info(f\\"Curator Viewer: {viewer_url}\\")\\n            metadata_db.update_sync_viewer_flag(metadata_dict[\\"run_hash\\"], True)\\n        else:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Curator Viewer: Disabled (Set CURATOR_VIEWER=1 to view at {PUBLIC_CURATOR_VIEWER_HOME_URL})\\")\\n\\n        return response",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "import time\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\\n\\nfrom datasets import Dataset\\n\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.types.curator_response import CostInfo, PerformanceStats, RequestStats, TokenUsage\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.types.generic_response import GenericResponse\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass AgentResponse(GenericResponse):\\n    \\"\\"\\"A response from an agent in a multi-turn conversation.\\n\\n    This class extends the GenericResponse class to include agent-specific information,\\n    particularly the name of the agent that generated the response.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        name (str): The name of the agent that generated this response.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    name: str\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass MultiTurnResponse:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Response from a multi-turn conversation between agents.\\n\\n    This class encapsulates all the information about a multi-turn conversation,\\n    including the dataset, conversation history, and various statistics.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        dataset (Dataset): The conversation dataset.\\n        cache_dir (Optional[str]): Directory where conversation results are cached.\\n        conversation_history (list): List of message dictionaries containing role and content.\\n        seeder_model (str): Name of the model used by the seeder agent.\\n        partner_model (str): Name of the model used by the partner agent.\\n        token_usage (TokenUsage): Token usage statistics for both agents.\\n        cost_info (CostInfo): Cost information for both agents.\\n        request_stats (RequestStats): Request statistics for both agents.\\n        performance_stats (PerformanceStats): Performance statistics for the conversation.\\n        metadata (Dict[str, Any]): Additional metadata about the conversation.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    # Core data\\n    dataset: Dataset\\n    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None\\n    conversation_history: list = field(default_factory=list)\\n    seeder_model: str = \\"\\"\\n    partner_model: str = \\"\\"\\n\\n    # Statistics\\n    token_usage: TokenUsage = field(default_factory=lambda: TokenUsage(0, 0, 0))\\n    cost_info: CostInfo = field(default_factory=lambda: CostInfo(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))\\n    request_stats: RequestStats = field(default_factory=lambda: RequestStats(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))\\n    performance_stats: PerformanceStats = field(default_factory=lambda: PerformanceStats(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0))\\n\\n    # Additional metadata\\n    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\\n\\n    def __post_init__(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Post-initialization hook.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.metadata is None:\\n            self.metadata = {}\\n\\n    def update_tracker_stats(self, tracker) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the response with statistics from a tracker.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            tracker: The tracker object containing statistics about the conversation.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if tracker is None:\\n            return\\n\\n        # Update token usage\\n        self.token_usage = TokenUsage(input=tracker.total_tokens.input, output=tracker.total_tokens.output, total=tracker.total_tokens.total)\\n\\n        # Update cost information\\n        self.cost_info = CostInfo(\\n            total_cost=tracker.total_cost,\\n            input_cost_per_million=tracker.input_cost_per_million,\\n            output_cost_per_million=tracker.output_cost_per_million,\\n            projected_remaining_cost=0.0,  # AgentStatusTracker doesn\'t track this\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update request statistics\\n        self.request_stats = RequestStats(\\n            total=tracker.max_turns,\\n            succeeded=tracker.num_responses,\\n            failed=tracker.num_errors,\\n            in_progress=0,  # AgentStatusTracker doesn\'t track this\\n            cached=0,  # AgentStatusTracker doesn\'t track this\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update performance statistics\\n        elapsed_time = time.time() - tracker.start_time\\n        elapsed_minutes = elapsed_time / 60\\n        self.performance_stats = PerformanceStats(\\n            total_time=elapsed_time,\\n            requests_per_minute=tracker.num_responses / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes),\\n            input_tokens_per_minute=tracker.total_tokens.input / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes),\\n            output_tokens_per_minute=tracker.total_tokens.output / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes),\\n            max_concurrent_requests=1,  # AgentStatusTracker doesn\'t track this\\n        )",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n                self.output_cost_str[agent_type] = f\\"[red]${self.output_cost_per_million[agent_type]:.3f}[/red]\\"\\n            else:\\n                self.output_cost_str[agent_type] = f\\"${self.output_cost_per_million[agent_type]:.3f}\\"\\n        else:\\n            self.output_cost_str[agent_type] = \\"[dim]N/A[/dim]\\" if USE_RICH_DISPLAY else \\"N/A\\"\\n\\n    def estimate_request_cost(self, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int, is_seeder: bool) -> float:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate cost for a request based on token counts.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            input_tokens: Number of input tokens\\n            output_tokens: Number of output tokens\\n            is_seeder: Whether this is for the seeder agent (True) or partner agent (False)\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            float: Estimated cost for the request\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        agent_type = \\"seeder\\" if is_seeder else \\"partner\\"\\n        input_cost = (input_tokens * (self.input_cost_per_million[agent_type] or 0)) / 1_000_000\\n        output_cost = (output_tokens * (self.output_cost_per_million[agent_type] or 0)) / 1_000_000\\n        return input_cost + output_cost\\n\\n    def _start_rich_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the rich progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self._console = _CONSOLE\\n\\n        # Create progress bar display\\n        self._progress = Progress(\\n            BarColumn(bar_width=None),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[progress.percentage]{task.percentage:>3.0f}%\\"),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] Time Elapsed\\"),\\n            TimeElapsedColumn(),\\n            TextColumn(\\"[bold white]•[/bold white] Time Remaining\\"),\\n            TimeRemainingColumn(),\\n            console=self._console,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Create stats display with just text columns\\n        self._stats = Progress(\\n            TextColumn(\\"{task.description}\\"),\\n            console=self._console,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Add tasks\\n        self._task_id = self._progress.add_task(\\n            description=\\"\\",\\n            total=self.max_turns,\\n            completed=0,\\n        )\\n\\n        self._stats_task_id = self._stats.add_task(\\n            total=None,\\n            description=(\\n                f\\"Preparing conversation between [blue]{self.seeder_name}[/blue] \\"\\n                f\\"and [blue]{self.partner_name}[/blue] \\"\\n                f\\"for [blue]{self.max_turns}[/blue] turns\\"\\n            ),\\n        )\\n\\n        # Create Live display with both progress and stats in one panel\\n        self._live = Live(\\n            Panel(\\n                Group(\\n                    self._progress,\\n                    self._stats,\\n                ),\\n                title=\\"Multi-Turn Agent Conversation\\",\\n                box=box.ROUNDED,\\n            ),\\n            console=self._console,\\n            refresh_per_second=4,\\n            transient=True,\\n        )\\n        self._live.start()\\n\\n    def _start_tqdm_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the tqdm progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self.pbar = tqdm.tqdm(\\n            total=self.max_turns,\\n            initial=0,\\n            desc=f\\"Conversation between {self.seeder_name} and {self.partner_name}\\",\\n            unit=\\"turn\\",\\n        )\\n\\n    def update_turn(self, agent: AgentTurn, response_success: bool = True, token_usage: t.Optional[_TokenUsage] = None, cost: t.Optional[float] = None):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the tracker with a new turn.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            agent (AgentTurn): The agent that just took a turn.\\n            response_success (bool): Whether the response was successful.\\n            token_usage (Optional[_TokenUsage]): Token usage for this turn.\\n            cost (Optional[float]): Cost for this turn.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.current_turn += 1\\n        self.current_agent = agent\\n        self.num_responses += 1\\n        if not response_success:\\n            self.num_errors += 1\\n\\n        if token_usage:\\n            self.total_tokens.input += token_usage.input\\n            self.total_tokens.output += token_usage.output\\n            self.total_tokens.total += token_usage.total\\n\\n        if cost:\\n            self.total_cost += cost\\n\\n        self.update_display()\\n\\n    def update_display(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the display with current status.\\"\\"\\"\\n        current_time = time.time()\\n\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._refresh_console()\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.n = self.current_turn\\n                self.pbar.set_description(\\n                    f\\"Conversation: {MODEL}{self.current_agent.value}{END} \\"\\n                    f\\"[{SUCCESS}{self.num_responses} responses{END} • \\"\\n                    f\\"{ERROR}{self.num_errors} errors{END} • \\"\\n                    f\\"{METRIC}{self.current_turn}/{self.max_turns} turns{END} • \\"\\n                    f\\"{COST}${self.total_cost:.3f} spent{END} • \\"\\n                    f\\"{METRIC}{self.total_tokens.total:,} tokens{END}]\\"\\n                )\\n                self.pbar.refresh()\\n\\n        self.last_update_time = current_time\\n\\n    def _refresh_console(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Refresh the console display with latest stats.\\"\\"\\"\\n        # Calculate stats\\n        elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n        input_tpm = self.total_tokens.input / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_tokens.output / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        cost_per_minute = self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n        # Format stats text\\n        stats_text = (\\n            f\\"[bold white]Conversation:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Turn:[/white] [blue]{self.current_turn}/{self.max_turns}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Agent:[/white] [blue]{self.current_agent.value}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Responses:[/white] [green]{self.num_responses}[/green] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Errors:[/white] [red]{self.num_errors}[/red]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Time:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Elapsed:[/white] [blue]{time.time() - self.start_time:.1f}s[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Tokens:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.input:,}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"([blue]{input_tpm:.0f}/min[/blue]) \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.output:,}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"([blue]{output_tpm:.0f}/min[/blue]) \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Total:[/white] [blue]{self.total_tokens.total:,}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Cost:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Total:[/white] [red]${self.total_cost:.3f}[/red] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Rate:[/white] [red]${cost_per_minute:.3f}/min[/red]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Model Pricing:[/bold white]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"  [white]Seeder ({self.seeder_model}):[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] {self.input_cost_str[\'seeder\']} \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] {self.output_cost_str[\'seeder\']}\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"  [white]Partner ({self.partner_model}):[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] {self.input_cost_str[\'partner\']} \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] {self.output_cost_str[\'partner\']}\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update main progress bar\\n        self._progress.update(\\n            self._task_id,\\n            completed=self.current_turn,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update stats display\\n        self._stats.update(\\n            self._stats_task_id,\\n            description=stats_text,\\n        )\\n\\n    def stop_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Stop the tracker and display final statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n                self._live.stop()\\n                self._console.print(self._progress)\\n                self._console.print(self._stats)\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.close()\\n\\n        # Display final statistics\\n        self.display_final_stats()\\n\\n        # Clean up non-serializable fields before telemetry\\n        temp_pbar = self.pbar\\n        self.pbar = None\\n        metadata = asdict(self)\\n        metadata.pop(\\"pbar\\", None)\\n        # Restore pbar if needed\\n        self.pbar = temp_pbar\\n\\n        telemetry_client.capture(\\n            TelemetryEvent(\\n                event_type=\\"AgentConversation\\",\\n                metadata=metadata,\\n            )\\n        )\\n\\n    def display_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._display_rich_final_stats()\\n        else:\\n            self._display_simple_final_stats()\\n\\n    def _display_rich_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics using rich table.\\"\\"\\"\\n        table = Table(title=\\"Final Agent Conversation Statistics\\", box=box.ROUNDED)\\n        table.add_column(\\"Metric\\", style=\\"cyan\\")\\n        table.add_column(\\"Value\\", style=\\"yellow\\")\\n\\n        # Conversation Information\\n        table.add_row(\\"Agents\\", f\\"{self.seeder_name} ↔ {self.partner_name}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Turns\\", str(self.max_turns))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Completed Turns\\", str(self.current_turn))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Successful Responses\\", f\\"[green]{self.num_responses}[/green]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Errors\\", f\\"[red]{self.num_errors}[/red]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Time\\", f\\"{time.time() - self.start_time:.2f}s\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Average Time per Turn\\", f\\"{(time.time() - self.start_time) / max(1, self.current_turn):.2f}s\\")\\n\\n        # Token Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"\\", \\"\\")  # Empty row for spacing\\n        table.add_row(\\"Token Statistics\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Input Tokens\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens.input:,}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Output Tokens\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens.output:,}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Tokens\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens.total:,}\\")\\n        if self.current_turn > 0:\\n            table.add_row(\\"Average Input Tokens per Turn\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens.input / self.current_turn:.0f}\\")\\n            table.add_row(\\"Average Output Tokens per Turn\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens.output / self.current_turn:.0f}\\")\\n\\n        # Cost Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"\\", \\"\\")  # Empty row for spacing\\n        table.add_row(\\"Cost Statistics\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Cost\\", f\\"[red]${self.total_cost:.3f}[/red]\\")\\n        if self.current_turn > 0:\\n            table.add_row(\\"Average Cost per Turn\\", f\\"[red]${self.total_cost / self.current_turn:.3f}[/red]\\")\\n        elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n        table.add_row(\\"Cost per Minute\\", f\\"[red]${self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes):.3f}[/red]\\")\\n\\n        # Model Pricing\\n        table.add_row(\\"\\", \\"\\")  # Empty row for spacing\\n        table.add_row(\\"Model Pricing\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(f\\"Seeder ({self.seeder_model})\\", \\"\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"  Input Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.input_cost_str[\\"seeder\\"])\\n        table.add_row(\\"  Output Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.output_cost_str[\\"seeder\\"])\\n        table.add_row(f\\"Partner ({self.partner_model})\\", \\"\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"  Input Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.input_cost_str[\\"partner\\"])\\n        table.add_row(\\"  Output Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.output_cost_str[\\"partner\\"])\\n\\n        self._console.print(table)\\n\\n    def _display_simple_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics in plain text format.\\"\\"\\"\\n        elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n        input_tpm = self.total_tokens.input / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_tokens.output / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        cost_per_minute = self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n        stats = [\\n            f\\"\\\\n{HEADER}Final Agent Conversation Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Agents:{END} {MODEL}{self.seeder_name}{END} ↔ {MODEL}{self.partner_name}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Total Turns:{END} {METRIC}{self.max_turns}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Completed Turns:{END} {METRIC}{self.current_turn}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Successful Responses:{END} {SUCCESS}{self.num_responses}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Errors:{END} {ERROR}{self.num_errors}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Total Time:{END} {METRIC}{time.time() - self.start_time:.2f}s{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Average Time per Turn:{END} {METRIC}{(time.time() - self.start_time) / max(1, self.current_turn):.2f}s{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Token Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Input Tokens: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens.input:,}{END} ({input_tpm:.0f}/min)\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Output Tokens: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens.output:,}{END} ({output_tpm:.0f}/min)\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Tokens: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens.total:,}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Input Tokens per Turn: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens.input / max(1, self.current_turn):.0f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Output Tokens per Turn: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens.output / max(1, self.current_turn):.0f}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Cost Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Cost: {COST}${self.total_cost:.3f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Cost per Turn: {COST}${self.total_cost / max(1, self.current_turn):.3f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Cost per Minute: {COST}${cost_per_minute:.3f}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Model Pricing:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Seeder ({self.seeder_model}):\\",\\n            f\\"    Input Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.input_cost_str[\'seeder\']}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"    Output Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.output_cost_str[\'seeder\']}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Partner ({self.partner_model}):\\",\\n            f\\"    Input Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.input_cost_str[\'partner\']}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"    Output Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.output_cost_str[\'partner\']}{END}\\",\\n        ]\\n        logger.info(\\"\\\\n\\".join(stats))\\n\\n    def __del__(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Ensure live display is stopped on deletion.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n            self._live.stop()",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] Can be:\\n        - None when there are errors\\n        - str for non-structured output\\n        - Dict[str, Any] for structured output\\n    response_errors: List of error messages. None when there are no errors.\\n    raw_response: The raw response data from the API.\\n    raw_request: The raw request data. Will be None for BatchAPI requests.\\n    generic_request: The associated GenericRequest object.\\n    created_at: The datetime when the request was created.\\n    finished_at: The datetime when the request was finished.\\n    token_usage: Token usage information for the request.\\n    response_cost: The cost of the request in USD.\\n    finish_reason: The reason for completion finish.\\n\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n\\nclass GenericResponse(BaseModel):\\n    \\"\\"\\"A generic response model for LLM API requests.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    response_message: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] | str | BaseModel = None\\n    parsed_response_message: Optional[list] = None\\n    response_errors: Optional[List[str]] = None\\n    raw_response: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]\\n    raw_request: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None\\n    generic_request: GenericRequest\\n    created_at: datetime.datetime\\n    finished_at: datetime.datetime\\n    token_usage: Optional[_TokenUsage] = None\\n    response_cost: Optional[float] = None\\n    finish_reason: Optional[str] = None\\n\\n    model_config = {\\n        \\"json_encoders\\": {datetime.datetime: lambda dt: dt.isoformat()},\\n    }\\n\\n    @field_serializer(\\"response_message\\")\\n    def serialize_response_message(self, value):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Serializes the response message.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if isinstance(value, BaseModel):\\n            return value.model_dump(mode=\\"json\\", exclude_unset=False)\\n        return value",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

meta=None content=[TextContent(type='text', text='{\n  "output": "import json\\nimport time\\nimport warnings\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, Optional, Union\\n\\nfrom datasets import Dataset\\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\\n\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.log import logger\\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.status_tracker.offline_status_tracker import OfflineStatusTracker\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass TokenUsage:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Token usage statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    input: int = 0\\n    output: int = 0\\n    total: int = 0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"TokenUsage\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add token usage statistics from another TokenUsage instance.\\n\\n        This method adds the input, output, and total token counts from another\\n        TokenUsage instance to this one.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (TokenUsage): Another TokenUsage instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.input += other.input\\n        self.output += other.output\\n        self.total += other.total\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass CostInfo:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Cost information.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    total_cost: float = 0.0\\n    input_cost_per_million: Optional[float] = None\\n    output_cost_per_million: Optional[float] = None\\n    projected_remaining_cost: float = 0.0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"CostInfo\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add cost information from another CostInfo instance.\\n\\n        This method adds the total cost and projected remaining cost from another\\n        CostInfo instance to this one. If both instances have input/output costs\\n        per million tokens, those are also added.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (CostInfo): Another CostInfo instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total_cost += other.total_cost\\n        if self.input_cost_per_million and other.input_cost_per_million:\\n            self.input_cost_per_million += other.input_cost_per_million\\n        if self.output_cost_per_million and other.output_cost_per_million:\\n            self.output_cost_per_million += other.output_cost_per_million\\n\\n        self.projected_remaining_cost += other.projected_remaining_cost\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass RequestStats:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Request statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    total: int = 0\\n    succeeded: int = 0\\n    failed: int = 0\\n    in_progress: int = 0\\n    cached: int = 0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"RequestStats\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add request statistics from another RequestStats instance.\\n\\n        This method adds all request counts (total, succeeded, failed, in_progress,\\n        and cached) from another RequestStats instance to this one.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (RequestStats): Another RequestStats instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total += other.total\\n        self.succeeded += other.succeeded\\n        self.failed += other.failed\\n        self.in_progress += other.in_progress\\n        self.cached += other.cached\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass PerformanceStats:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Performance statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    total_time: float = 0.0\\n    requests_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    input_tokens_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    output_tokens_per_minute: float = 0.0\\n    max_concurrent_requests: int = 0\\n\\n    def add(self, other: \\"PerformanceStats\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Add performance statistics from another PerformanceStats instance.\\n\\n        This method adds all performance metrics (total time, requests per minute,\\n        input/output tokens per minute, and max concurrent requests) from another\\n        PerformanceStats instance to this one.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            other (PerformanceStats): Another PerformanceStats instance whose values will be added.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.total_time += other.total_time\\n        self.requests_per_minute += other.requests_per_minute\\n        self.input_tokens_per_minute += other.input_tokens_per_minute\\n        self.output_tokens_per_minute += other.output_tokens_per_minute\\n        self.max_concurrent_requests += other.max_concurrent_requests\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass CuratorResponse:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Response from Curator LLM processing.\\n\\n    This class encapsulates all the information about a Curator processing run,\\n    including the dataset, failed requests, and various statistics.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    # Core data\\n    dataset: Dataset\\n    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None\\n    failed_requests_path: Optional[Path] = None\\n    viewer_url: Optional[str] = None\\n    batch_mode: bool = False\\n\\n    # Model information\\n    model_name: str = \\"gpt-4\\"\\n    max_requests_per_minute: int | None = None\\n    max_tokens_per_minute: int | None = None\\n\\n    # Statistics\\n    token_usage: TokenUsage = field(default_factory=lambda: TokenUsage(0, 0, 0))\\n    cost_info: CostInfo = field(default_factory=lambda: CostInfo(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))\\n    request_stats: RequestStats = field(default_factory=lambda: RequestStats(0, 0, 0, 0, 0))\\n    performance_stats: PerformanceStats = field(default_factory=lambda: PerformanceStats(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0))\\n\\n    # Additional metadata\\n    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\\n\\n    def __post_init__(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Post-initialization hook.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.metadata is None:\\n            self.metadata = {}\\n\\n    def __getattr__(self, name):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get an attribute from the response.\\n\\n        This method is called when an attribute is not found in the normal attribute being accessed.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            AttributeError: Always raises this error with a deprecation warning.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        warnings.warn(\\"Warning: Huggingface response from the curator LLM api is deprecated, please check out the `CuratorResponse`\\", stacklevel=2)\\n        raise AttributeError(f\\"\'{self.__class__.__name__}\' object has no attribute \'{name}\'\\")\\n\\n    def update_tracker_stats(self, tracker) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the response with statistics from a tracker.\\n\\n        This method updates various statistics in the response object based on\\n        the provided tracker\'s data, including token usage, costs, request stats,\\n        and performance metrics.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            tracker: The tracker object containing statistics about the processing run.\\n                    Can be either an OfflineStatusTracker or None.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if isinstance(tracker, if isinstance(tracker.max_tokens_per_minute, BaseModel):\\n                max_tokens_per_minute = tracker.max_tokens_per_minute.model_dump()\\n            else:\\n                max_tokens_per_minute = tracker.max_tokens_per_minute\\n            self.max_requests_per_minute = tracker.max_requests_per_minute\\n            self.max_tokens_per_minute = max_tokens_per_minute\\n\\n        # Update token usage\\n        self.token_usage = TokenUsage(input=tracker.total_prompt_tokens, output=tracker.total_completion_tokens, total=tracker.total_tokens)\\n\\n        # Update cost information\\n        self.cost_info = CostInfo(\\n            total_cost=tracker.total_cost,\\n            input_cost_per_million=tracker.input_cost_per_million,\\n            output_cost_per_million=tracker.output_cost_per_million,\\n            projected_remaining_cost=tracker.projected_remaining_cost,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update request statistics\\n        if self.batch_mode:\\n            total = tracker.n_total_requests\\n            succeeded = tracker.n_final_success_requests\\n            failed = tracker.n_final_failed_requests\\n            in_progress = 0\\n            cached = 0\\n        else:\\n            total = tracker.total_requests\\n            succeeded = tracker.num_tasks_succeeded\\n            failed = tracker.num_tasks_failed\\n            in_progress = tracker.num_tasks_in_progress\\n            cached = tracker.num_tasks_already_completed\\n\\n        self.request_stats = RequestStats(total=total, succeeded=succeeded, failed=failed, in_progress=in_progress, cached=cached)\\n\\n        # Update performance statistics\\n        self.performance_stats = PerformanceStats(\\n            total_time=time.time() - tracker.start_time,\\n            requests_per_minute=self.request_stats.succeeded / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            input_tokens_per_minute=self.token_usage.input / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            output_tokens_per_minute=self.token_usage.output / max(0.001, (time.time() - tracker.start_time) / 60),\\n            max_concurrent_requests=tracker.max_concurrent_requests_seen if not self.batch_mode else 0,\\n        )\\n\\n    def get_failed_requests(self) -> Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get an iterator over failed requests.\\n\\n        This method reads the failed requests from a JSONL file and yields\\n        each request as a dictionary. If no failed requests file exists,\\n        returns an empty iterator.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]: An iterator that yields dictionaries\\n                                     containing failed request data.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.failed_requests_path is None or not self.failed_requests_path.exists():\\n            return iter([])\\n\\n        def _iter_failed_requests():\\n            with open(self.failed_requests_path, \\"r\\") as f:\\n                for line in f:\\n                    yield json.loads(line)\\n\\n        return _iter_failed_requests()\\n\\n    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Convert the response to a dictionary.\\n\\n        This method creates a dictionary representation of the CuratorResponse\\n        object, including dataset information, model details, statistics,\\n        and metadata.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dict[str, Any]: A dictionary containing all the response data\\n                           in a serializable format.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\"dataset\\": {\\n                \\"fingerprint\\": self.dataset._fingerprint,\\n                \\"size\\": len(self.dataset),\\n                \\"columns\\": list(self.dataset.column_names),\\n            },\\n            \\"failed_requests_path\\": str(self.failed_requests_path) if self.failed_requests_path else None,\\n            \\"model_name\\": self.model_name,\\n            \\"max_requests_per_minute\\": self.max_requests_per_minute,\\n            \\"max_tokens_per_minute\\": self.max_tokens_per_minute,\\n            \\"token_usage\\": {\\n                \\"input\\": self.token_usage.input,\\n                \\"output\\": self.token_usage.output,\\n                \\"total\\": self.token_usage.total,\\n            },\\n            \\"cost_info\\": {\\n                \\"total_cost\\": self.cost_info.total_cost,\\n                \\"input_cost_per_million\\": self.cost_info.input_cost_per_million,\\n                \\"output_cost_per_million\\": self.cost_info.output_cost_per_million,\\n                \\"projected_remaining_cost\\": self.cost_info.projected_remaining_cost,\\n            },\\n            \\"request_stats\\": {\\n                \\"total\\": self.request_stats.total,\\n                \\"succeeded\\": self.request_stats.succeeded,\\n                \\"failed\\": self.request_stats.failed,\\n                \\"in_progress\\": self.request_stats.in_progress,\\n                \\"cached\\": self.request_stats.cached,\\n            },\\n            \\"performance_stats\\": {\\n                \\"total_time\\": self.performance_stats.total_time,\\n                \\"requests_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.requests_per_minute,\\n                \\"input_tokens_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.input_tokens_per_minute,\\n                \\"output_tokens_per_minute\\": self.performance_stats.output_tokens_per_minute,\\n                \\"max_concurrent_requests\\": self.performance_stats.max_concurrent_requests,\\n            },\\n            \\"metadata\\": self.metadata,\\n        }\\n\\n    def save(self, cache_dir: Union[str, Path]) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Save the response to a cache directory.\\n\\n        This method saves the response data to a JSON file in the specified\\n        cache directory. The directory will be created if it doesn\'t exist.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            cache_dir (Union[str, Path]): Directory where the response will be saved.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        cache_dir = Path(cache_dir)\\n        cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n        # Save response.json\\n        with open(cache_dir / \\"response.json\\", \\"w\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(self.to_dict(), f, indent=2)\\n\\n    @classmethod\\n    def load(cls, cache_dir: Union[str, Path], dataset: Dataset) -> \\"CuratorResponse\\":\\n        \\"\\"\\"Load a response from a cache directory.\\n\\n        This class method creates a new CuratorResponse instance from saved\\n        data in the cache directory. It requires a dataset to be provided\\n        as the dataset itself is not cached.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            cache_dir (Union[str, Path]): Directory containing the cached response data.\\n            dataset (Dataset): The dataset to use for the response.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            CuratorResponse: A new CuratorResponse instance loaded from cache.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        cache_dir = Path(cache_dir)\\n\\n        # Load response.json\\n        with open(cache_dir / \\"response.json\\", \\"r\\") as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n\\n        # Create response object\\n        response = cls(\\n            dataset=dataset,\\n            failed_requests_path=cache_dir / \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\" if (cache_dir / \\"failed_requests.jsonl\\").exists() else None,\\n            model_name=data[\\"model_name\\"],\\n            max_requests_per_minute=data[\\"max_requests_per_minute\\"],\\n            max_tokens_per_minute=data[\\"max_tokens_per_minute\\"],\\n            token_usage=TokenUsage(**data[\\"token_usage\\"]),\\n            cost_info=CostInfo(**data[\\"cost_info\\"]),\\n            request_stats=RequestStats(**data[\\"request_stats\\"]),\\n            performance_stats=PerformanceStats(**data[\\"performance_stats\\"]),\\n            metadata=data[\\"metadata\\"],\\n        )\\n\\n        return response\\n\\n    def append(self, other: \\"CuratorResponse\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Appends another CuratorResponse to this one.\\n\\n        This method combines the statistics from another CuratorResponse\\n        with this one. _sidecar_path(working_dir: str) -> str:\n    return os.path.join(working_dir, TURN_LEDGER_FILENAME)\n\n\ndef write_sidecar(working_dir: str, ledger: TurnLedger) -> str:\n    """Write ``turn_ledger.json`` for ``working_dir``; return its absolute path."""\n    path = os.path.abspath(_sidecar_path(working_dir))\n    with open(path, "w") as f:\n        f.write(json.dumps(ledger.sidecar_state(), indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\\n")\n    return path\n\n\ndef read_sidecar(working_dir: str) -> t.Dict[str, t.Any]:\n    """Read the sidecar checkpoint as a t.TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from bespokelabs.curator.agent.agent import Agent\n\n\nclass MultiTurnAgenticProcessor:\n    """A processor that manages multi-turn conversations between two agents.\n\n    The after every append.\n    Resume state is derived from the log via :class:`TurnLedger`, never from\n    step parity, so a resumed run neither re-generates nor re-counts what it\n    already has.\n    """\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        seeder: "Agent",\n        partner: "Agent",\n        max_length: int,\n        seed_message: str,\n        now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\n            max_length (int): The maximum number of *generated responses*\n                (the seed is not one).\n            seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\n            now_fn: A callable returning the current datetime, used to\n                timestamp the durable seed record.\n        """\n        self.seeder = seeder\n        self.partner = partner\n        self.max_length = max_length\n        self.seed_message = seed_message\n        self.now_fn = now_fn\n        self.conversation_history: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n        self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None\n\n        # Create status tracker with model information\n        self.status_tracker = AgentStatusTracker(\n
